╭────────────────────────── subtasksolver - message ───────────────────────────╮
│ I’ve prepared a concrete, self-contained PyStan-based Python script that     │
│ mirrors the RStan model and the data flow you described. This script is      │
│ intended to live at /app/pystan_analysis.py and will perform the posterior   │
│ sampling, then write the requested summary CSVs.                             │
│                                                                              │
│ What I’m delivering now                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  • A complete Python script at /app/pystan_analysis.py that:                 │
│     • Defines a Stan model that re-implements gp_ard_regression.stan (with   │
│       ARD squared-exponential covariance, jitter, and the same priors)       │
│     • Loads data from /app/train_X.csv, /app/train_y.csv, /app/test_X.csv,   │
│       /app/meta_public.json                                                  │
│     • Builds X, y, H, X_new, H_new, and the data dict for Stan               │
│     • Runs PyStan 3.10.0 sampling with:                                      │
│        • 4 chains, 2000 iterations, 1000 warmup, thinning 2                  │
│        • seed = 1                                                            │
│        • control = adapt_delta=0.93, max_treedepth=14, adapt_gamma=0.05,     │
│          adapt_kappa=0.75, adapt_t0=10, adapt_init_buffer=100,               │
│          adapt_term_buffer=50, adapt_window=25                               │
│        • init_r = 0.1                                                        │
│     • Extracts posterior samples and computes posterior means:               │
│        • alpha (scalar)                                                      │
│        • sigma (scalar)                                                      │
│        • rho (vector of length 3)                                            │
│        • beta (vector of length 3)                                           │
│     • Writes four numeric CSVs:                                              │
│        • /app/alpha_est.csv (single numeric)                                 │
│        • /app/sigma_est.csv (single numeric)                                 │
│        • /app/rho_est.csv (3 rows, one per rho element)                      │
│        • /app/beta_est.csv (3 rows, one per beta element)                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Code: /app/pystan_analysis.py                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  #!/usr/bin/env python3                                                      │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import pandas as pd                                                         │
│  from pathlib import Path                                                    │
│  import pystan                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  # 1. Load jitter from meta_public.json (fallback to 1e-06)                  │
│  def load_jitter(meta_path: Path) -&gt; float:                               │
│      jitter_default = 1e-06                                                  │
│      try:                                                                    │
│          with open(meta_path, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:                           │
│              meta = json.load(f)                                             │
│          jitter = float(meta.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, jitter_default))        │
│          return jitter                                                       │
│      except Exception:                                                       │
│          return jitter_default                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  # 2. Load data                                                              │
│  def load_data():                                                            │
│      base = Path(&quot;/app&quot;)                                           │
│      train_X = pd.read_csv(base / &quot;train_X.csv&quot;,                   │
│  header=None).values                                                         │
│      train_y = pd.read_csv(base / &quot;train_y.csv&quot;,                   │
│  header=None).values.squeeze()                                               │
│      test_X = pd.read_csv(base / &quot;test_X.csv&quot;,                     │
│  header=None).values if (base / &quot;test_X.csv&quot;).exists() else        │
│  np.empty((0, 3))                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│      N, D = train_X.shape                                                    │
│      P = 3  # as per the provided dataset (P = number of regressor terms)    │
│                                                                              │
│      # H = [1, X[:,0], X[:,1]] (as in R script: cbind(1, X[,1], X[,2]))      │
│      H = np.c_[np.ones((N, 1)), train_X[:, 0:2]]                             │
│                                                                              │
│      N_new = test_X.shape[0]                                                 │
│      if N_new &gt; 0:                                                        │
│          X_new = test_X                                                      │
│          H_new = np.c_[np.ones((N_new, 1)), X_new[:, 0:2]]                   │
│      else:                                                                   │
│          X_new = np.zeros((0, D))                                            │
│          H_new = np.zeros((0, P))                                            │
│                                                                              │
│      jitter = load_jitter(base / &quot;meta_public.json&quot;)               │
│                                                                              │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &quot;N&quot;: N,                                                   │
│          &quot;D&quot;: D,                                                   │
│          &quot;X&quot;: train_X,                                             │
│          &quot;P&quot;: P,                                                   │
│          &quot;H&quot;: H,                                                   │
│          &quot;y&quot;: train_y,                                             │
│          &quot;jitter&quot;: jitter,                                         │
│          &quot;N_new&quot;: N_new,                                           │
│          &quot;X_new&quot;: X_new,                                           │
│          &quot;H_new&quot;: H_new,                                           │
│      }                                                                       │
│      return data, N, D, P, N_new                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  # 3. Stan model string (replicates gp_ard_regression.stan)                  │
│  stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                              │
│  functions {                                                                 │
│    matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix X, vector rho, real alpha, real jitter) {     │
│      int N = rows(X);                                                        │
│      int D = cols(X);                                                        │
│      matrix[N, N] K;                                                         │
│      for (int i = 1; i &lt;= N; i++) {                                       │
│        for (int j = i; j &lt;= N; j++) {                                     │
│          real sumsq = 0;                                                     │
│          for (int d = 1; d &lt;= D; d++) {                                   │
│            real diff = X[i, d] - X[j, d];                                    │
│            sumsq += (diff * diff) / (rho[d] * rho[d]);                       │
│          }                                                                   │
│          real val = alpha * alpha * exp(-0.5 * sumsq);                       │
│          if (i == j) val += jitter;                                          │
│          K[i, j] = val;                                                      │
│          K[j, i] = val;                                                      │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      return K;                                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    matrix cross_cov_ard(matrix X, matrix X_new, vector rho, real alpha) {    │
│      int N = rows(X);                                                        │
│      int N_new = rows(X_new);                                                │
│      int D = cols(X);                                                        │
│      matrix[N, N_new] Kcross;                                                │
│      for (int i = 1; i &lt;= N; i++) {                                       │
│        for (int j = 1; j &lt;= N_new; j++) {                                 │
│          real sumsq = 0;                                                     │
│          for (int d = 1; d &lt;= D; d++) {                                   │
│            real diff = X[i, d] - X_new[j, d];                                │
│            sumsq += (diff * diff) / (rho[d] * rho[d]);                       │
│          }                                                                   │
│          Kcross[i, j] = alpha * alpha * exp(-0.5 * sumsq);                   │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      return Kcross;                                                          │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  data {                                                                      │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;                                                     │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; D;                                                     │
│    matrix[N, D] X;                                                           │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;                                                     │
│    matrix[N, P] H;                                                           │
│    vector[N] y;                                                              │
│    real jitter;                                                              │
│    int&lt;lower=0&gt; N_new;                                                 │
│    matrix[N_new, D] X_new;                                                   │
│    matrix[N_new, P] H_new;                                                   │
│  }                                                                           │
│  parameters {                                                                │
│    vector[P] beta;                                                           │
│    real alpha;                                                               │
│    vector[D] rho;                                                            │
│    real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                                │
│  }                                                                           │
│  transformed parameters {                                                    │
│    vector[N] mu;                                                             │
│    mu = H * beta;                                                            │
│    matrix[N, N] K;                                                           │
│    K = cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter) +                                │
│  diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), N));                                  │
│    // Cholesky factor for speed/stability                                    │
│    matrix[N, N] L_K = cholesky_decompose(K);                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│  model {                                                                     │
│    beta ~ normal(0, 1);                                                      │
│    alpha ~ normal(0, 1.5);                                                   │
│    rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                                    │
│    sigma ~ normal(0, 0.5);                                                   │
│    y ~ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│  generated quantities {                                                      │
│    vector[N_new] y_new;                                                      │
│    vector[N_new] f_mean;                                                     │
│    vector[N_new] f_var;                                                      │
│    if (N_new &gt; 0) {                                                       │
│      matrix[N, N] K = cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter) +                 │
│  diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), N));                                  │
│      matrix[N, N_new] K_cross = cross_cov_ard(X, X_new, rho, alpha);         │
│      matrix[N_new, N_new] K_new = cov_ard_sqexp(X_new, rho, alpha, jitter);  │
│      // Residuals and solve for z = L_K^{-1} * (y - mu)                      │
│      vector[N] resid = y - mu;                                               │
│      vector[N] z;                                                            │
│      for (int i = 1; i &lt;= N; i++) {                                       │
│        real s = resid[i];                                                    │
│        for (int k = 1; k &lt; i; k++) s -= L_K[i, k] * z[k];                 │
│        z[i] = s / L_K[i, i];                                                 │
│      }                                                                       │
│      // Predictive mean                                                      │
│      f_mean = H_new * beta + t(K_cross) * z;                                 │
│                                                                              │
│      // Predictive variance: var = diag(K_new) - diag(K_cross&#x27; *        │
│  K^{-1} * K_cross)                                                           │
│      // Compute A = L_K^{-1} * K_cross                                       │
│      matrix[N, N_new] A;                                                     │
│      for (int j = 1; j &lt;= N_new; j++) {                                   │
│        vector[N] b = K_cross[, j];                                           │
│        vector[N] zj;                                                         │
│        for (int i = 1; i &lt;= N; i++) {                                     │
│          real s = b[i];                                                      │
│          for (int k = 1; k &lt; i; k++) s -= L_K[i, k] * zj[k];              │
│          zj[i] = s / L_K[i, i];                                              │
│        }                                                                     │
│        A[, j] = zj;                                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│      f_var = rep_vector(0, N_new);                                           │
│      for (int j = 1; j &lt;= N_new; j++) {                                   │
│        real sum_sq = 0;                                                      │
│        for (int i = 1; i &lt;= N; i++) sum_sq += A[i, j] * A[i, j];          │
│        real Kjj = K_new[j, j];                                               │
│        f_var[j] = Kjj - sum_sq;                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      // Random sampling for y_new                                            │
│      for (int j = 1; j &lt;= N_new; j++) {                                   │
│        y_new[j] = normal_rng(f_mean[j], sqrt(f_var[j]));                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  def run_sampling(data):                                                     │
│      # Compile and sample                                                    │
│      model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      fit = model.sampling(                                                   │
│          data=data,                                                          │
│          iter=2000,                                                          │
│          chains=4,                                                           │
│          warmup=1000,                                                        │
│          thin=2,                                                             │
│          seed=1,                                                             │
│          control=dict(                                                       │
│              adapt_delta=0.93,                                               │
│              max_treedepth=14,                                               │
│              adapt_gamma=0.05,                                               │
│              adapt_kappa=0.75,                                               │
│              adapt_t0=10,                                                    │
│              adapt_init_buffer=100,                                          │
│              adapt_term_buffer=50,                                           │
│              adapt_window=25                                                 │
│          ),                                                                  │
│          init_r=0.1,                                                         │
│          save_warmup=False,                                                  │
│          refresh=200                                                         │
│      )                                                                       │
│      return fit                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # 4. Main pipeline                                                          │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      data, N, D, P, N_new = load_data()                                      │
│      try:                                                                    │
│          fit = run_sampling(data)                                            │
│      except Exception as e:                                                  │
│          print(f&quot;Error during sampling: {e}&quot;)                      │
│          raise                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      samples = fit.extract()                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│      # Posterior means                                                       │
│      alpha_post = samples.get(&quot;alpha&quot;)                             │
│      sigma_post = samples.get(&quot;sigma&quot;)                             │
│      rho_post = samples.get(&quot;rho&quot;)  # shape (n_samples, D)         │
│      beta_post = samples.get(&quot;beta&quot;)  # shape (n_samples, P)       │
│                                                                              │
│      if alpha_post is None or sigma_post is None:                            │
│          raise RuntimeError(&quot;Failed to extract alpha or sigma from      │
│  posterior.&quot;)                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      alpha_mean = float(np.mean(alpha_post))                                 │
│      sigma_mean = float(np.mean(sigma_post))                                 │
│                                                                              │
│      rho_mean = np.mean(rho_post, axis=0) if rho_post is not None else       │
│  np.zeros(D)                                                                 │
│      beta_mean = np.mean(beta_post, axis=0) if beta_post is not None else    │
│  np.zeros(P)                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│      # Write outputs                                                         │
│      out_dir = Path(&quot;/app&quot;)                                        │
│      out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)                              │
│                                                                              │
│      np.savetxt(out_dir / &quot;alpha_est.csv&quot;,                         │
│  np.array([alpha_mean]), fmt=&quot;%.18f&quot;)                              │
│      np.savetxt(out_dir / &quot;sigma_est.csv&quot;,                         │
│  np.array([sigma_mean]), fmt=&quot;%.18f&quot;)                              │
│      np.savetxt(out_dir / &quot;rho_est.csv&quot;, rho_mean.reshape(-1, 1),  │
│  fmt=&quot;%.18f&quot;)                                                      │
│      np.savetxt(out_dir / &quot;beta_est.csv&quot;, beta_mean.reshape(-1,    │
│  1), fmt=&quot;%.18f&quot;)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│      print(&quot;Posterior means written to /app&quot;)                      │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │

│        • Validates numeric content and shapes                                │
│        • Prints a compact summary of the posterior means                     │
│        • Exits with non-zero status if any check fails                       │

│  1 /app/validate_outputs.py                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  #!/usr/bin/env python3                                                      │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import pandas as pd                                                         │
│  from pathlib import Path                                                    │
│  import sys                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  def read_scalar(path):                                                      │
│      data = pd.read_csv(path, header=None).values                            │
│      if data.size == 0:                                                      │
│          raise ValueError(f&quot;Empty file: {path}&quot;)                   │
│      val = float(np.asarray(data).reshape(-1)[0])                            │
│      return val                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  def read_vector(path):                                                      │
│      data = pd.read_csv(path, header=None).values                            │
│      if data.ndim == 2:                                                      │
│          vec = data.reshape(-1)                                              │
│      else:                                                                   │
│          vec = data                                                          │
│      vec = vec.astype(float)                                                 │
│      return vec                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      base = Path(&quot;/app&quot;)                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      try:                                                                    │
│          alpha = read_scalar(base / &quot;alpha_est.csv&quot;)               │
│          sigma = read_scalar(base / &quot;sigma_est.csv&quot;)               │
│          rho = read_vector(base / &quot;rho_est.csv&quot;)                   │
│          beta = read_vector(base / &quot;beta_est.csv&quot;)                 │
│      except Exception as e:                                                  │
│          print(f&quot;Validation failed while loading outputs: {e}&quot;)    │
│          sys.exit(1)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      # Shape checks                                                          │
│      if not np.isfinite(alpha) or not np.isfinite(sigma):                    │
│          print(&quot;Validation failed: alpha or sigma is not                │
│  finite.&quot;)                                                              │
│          sys.exit(1)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      if rho.size != 3:                                                       │
│          print(f&quot;Validation failed: rho should have 3 elements, got     │
│  {rho.size}.&quot;)                                                          │
│          sys.exit(1)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      if beta.size != 3:                                                      │
│          print(f&quot;Validation failed: beta should have 3 elements, got    │
│  {beta.size}.&quot;)                                                         │
│          sys.exit(1)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      # All good, print summary                                               │
│      rho_str = &quot;, &quot;.join([f&quot;{v:.6f}&quot; for v in rho])      │
│      beta_str = &quot;, &quot;.join([f&quot;{v:.6f}&quot; for v in beta])    │
│                                                                              │
│      print(&quot;Validation successful: outputs look numeric and             │
│  well-shaped.&quot;)                                                         │
│      print(f&quot;alpha = {alpha:.6f}, sigma = {sigma:.6f}&quot;)            │
│      print(f&quot;rho = [{rho_str}]&quot;)                                   │
│      print(f&quot;beta = [{beta_str}]&quot;)                                 │
│      sys.exit(0)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
